    18: const daysFromToday = (dateText) => {
    19:   const due = new Date(`${dateText}T12:00:00`), now = new Date(`${dateKey(today)}T12:00:00`);
    20:   return Number.isNaN(due.getTime()) ? null : Math.round((due-now)/86400000);
    21: };

  1349: function calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(event) {
  1350:   const text = `${event?.title || ""} ${event?.target || ""} ${event?.detail || ""}`;
  1351:   if (/(정기고사|기말고사|중간고사|방학식|개학식|교직원회의|부장교사회의|운영위원회|석식\s*[Xx]|급식제공|시간표\s*운영|교실개방|생활기록부.*점검|입찰|폐지\(|폐기물|제안서.*제출기간)/.test(text)) return false;
  1352:   return /(프로그램|특강|체험|교육|설명회|공연|상담|탐방|캠프|연수|활동|동아리|수련|수학여행|대회|촬영|학습코칭|석학과의 만남)/.test(text);
  1353: }

  1678: function nightStudyOperationStatus(key){

  1680:   const rules=readNightStudyRules().filter(r=>r&&r.active!==false);
  1681:   for(const r of rules){
  1682:     if(r.type==='range'&&r.start&&r.end&&key>=r.start&&key<=r.end)return {operates:false,reason:r.name||'미운영 기간'};

  1684:   }
  1685:   return {operates:true,reason:'운영일'};

  1707: function dormRuleHistory(){
  1708:   const sheetRules=Array.isArray(readonlyCache?.dormRules)?readonlyCache.dormRules:[];

  1710:   return dormDefaultRules().map(x=>({...x,active:x.active!==false,source:'UEP 기본값(시트 연결 대기)'}));
  1711: }

  2843: function inputCenterClassifyMaterial(file={}, textValue=''){
  2844:   const name=String(file?.name||file?.fileName||'');
  2845:   const text=String(textValue||file?.text||'');
  2846:   const hay=`${name}\n${text.slice(0,12000)}`.toLowerCase();
  2847:   const rules=[
  2848:     {type:'프로그램 신청서',score:[/신청자|참가신청|신청 활동|신청 프로그램|희망 진로|희망순위|신청서/g,/연락처|학년|반|번호|이름/g],reason:'신청·희망·참가 관련 열/문구'},
  2849:     {type:'만족도·설문조사',score:[/만족도|설문|응답|매우 만족|보통|불만족|소감/g,/문항|응답자|제출시간|타임스탬프/g],reason:'설문·응답 관련 문구'},
  2850:     {type:'수행평가지',score:[/수행평가|서술형|평가지|배점|채점|문항|풀이/g,/교과|성취기준|평가요소/g],reason:'수행평가·채점 관련 문구'},
  2851:     {type:'보고서·탐구보고서',score:[/보고서|탐구|활동내용|새롭게 알게|느낀점|소감|주제/g,/책 제목|독서|진로희망|탐구내용/g],reason:'보고서·탐구·독서 관련 문구'},
  2852:     {type:'출결·참여명단',score:[/출석|결석|참여명단|참가자 명단|출결|출석부/g,/학번|이름|반/g],reason:'출결·참여명단 관련 문구'},
  2853:     {type:'성적·평가자료',score:[/성적|점수|등급|석차|평균|원점수|표준점수/g,/학번|이름|과목/g],reason:'성적·점수 관련 열/문구'},
  2854:     {type:'상담·관찰자료',score:[/상담|관찰|면담|상담내용|교사 의견|특이사항/g],reason:'상담·관찰 관련 문구'}
  2855:   ];
  2856:   let best={type:'기타',points:0,reason:'명확한 자동 분류 근거 없음'};
  2857:   for(const r of rules){let pts=0;for(const re of r.score){const m=hay.match(re);if(m)pts+=Math.min(4,m.length);}if(pts>best.points)best={type:r.type,points:pts,reason:r.reason};}
  2858:   const confidence=best.points>=6?'높음':best.points>=3?'보통':'낮음';
  2859:   return {...best,confidence};
  2860: }

  2961: function inputCenterProgramCandidates(purpose=inputCenterConfig.purpose,semester=inputCenterConfig.semester){
  2962:   const filtered=(readonlyCache?.programs||[]).filter(p=>{
  2963:     const group=inputCenterProgramGroup(p); if(!group) return false;
  2964:     const sem=inputCenterProgramSemester(p); if(sem&&semester&&sem!==String(semester)) return false;
  2965:     if(purpose==='보고서 연결') return (group==='common'||group==='selected') && p.reportRequired!==false && p.reportUse!==false;
  2966:     if(purpose==='프로그램 참가신청') return group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
  2967:     if(purpose==='만족도조사') return group==='common'||group==='selected'||group==='after'||group==='night';
  2968:     return false;
  2969:   });
  2970:   // readonly 프로그램 데이터에는 방과후 차시/일정 결합 때문에 같은 마스터 강좌가 여러 행으로 들어올 수 있습니다.
  2971:   // 입력센터 연결목록은 차시가 아니라 '프로그램 마스터 1건'을 선택하는 곳이므로 안정 ID 기준으로 1회만 노출합니다.
  2972:   const deduped=[]; const seen=new Set();

  4202: function accessAccountRole(account){
  4203:   if(String(account?.admin||'').toUpperCase()==='Y')return '관리자';
  4204:   const role=String(account?.role||'').trim();
  4205:   if(/학년부장/.test(role))return '학년부장';
  4206:   if(/학년총무/.test(role))return '학년총무';
  4207:   if(/담임/.test(role))return '담임교사';
  4208:   if(/교감/.test(role))return '교감';
  4209:   if(/교장/.test(role))return '교장';
  4210:   return '교과담당';
  4211: }

  5880: function recordDateText(program){

  5882:   const selectedDate=override?.confirmed&&override.date?override.date:"";

  5885:   if(start&&end&&start!==end)return `${fmt(start)}-${fmt(end)}`;
  5886:   return fmt(start||end);

  8677: function noticeCollectionState(item){

  9527: function parseReferencePaste(text, type) {
  9528:   const headers = {
  9529:     approval: ["업무분류", "업무 종류", "결재라인", "비고"],
  9530:     homeroom: ["학년", "반", "담임", "부담임"],

--- 9530 ---
  9525: }
  9526: 
  9527: function parseReferencePaste(text, type) {
  9528:   const headers = {
  9529:     approval: ["업무분류", "업무 종류", "결재라인", "비고"],
  9530:     homeroom: ["학년", "반", "담임", "부담임"],
  9531:     extension: ["구역", "이름", "시설", "자리", "대표번호", "내선"],
  9532:   }[type] || [];
  9533:   const widths = { approval: 3, homeroom: 4, extension: 4 };
  9534:   return String(text || "").split(/\r?\n/).map((line) => {
  9535:     const cells = (line.includes("\t") ? line.split("\t") : line.split(/\s{2,}|,/))
  9536:       .map((cell) => String(cell || "").trim());
  9537:     while (cells.length && !cells[cells.length - 1]) cells.pop();
  9538:     return cells.slice(0, widths[type] || cells.length);
  9539:   }).filter((row) => {
  9540:     const joined = row.join(" ");
  9541:     return row.filter(Boolean).length >= 2 && !headers.some((header) => joined === header || joined.startsWith(`${header} `));
  9542:   });

 11622: function openNightProgramSummary(id, studentKey="", day="", slot=""){
 11623:   const all=[...afterSchoolProgramGroups(),...groupedAfterSchoolCourses(afterSchoolProgramGroups()),...(readonlyCache?.programs||[])];
 11624:   const source=all.find(item=>String(item.id)===String(id));
 11625:   const program=programWithOverride(source);
 11626:   if(!program) return openAfterschoolProgramDrawer(id);
 11627:   const display=nightProgramStatus(program);
 11628:   const student=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(x=>attendanceStudentKey(x)===String(studentKey)||String(x.id)===String(studentKey)||String(x.studentNo)===String(studentKey));
 11629:   const participant=(program.students||[]).find(x=>String(x.studentId||"")===String(student?.id||"")||String(x.studentNo||x.number||"")===String(student?.studentNo||""));

   410: function googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt){
   411:   const identity=`${os.hostname()}|${os.userInfo().username}|UEP-GOOGLE-USER-OAUTH-v1`;
   412:   return crypto.scryptSync(identity,salt,32);

   414: function encodeGoogleUserOAuthRecovery(value){
   415:   const salt=crypto.randomBytes(16),iv=crypto.randomBytes(12),key=googleUserOAuthRecoveryKey(salt);
   416:   const cipher=crypto.createCipheriv('aes-256-gcm',key,iv);
   417:   const encrypted=Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value),'utf8'),cipher.final()]);

  1924: function neisDateToLocalDate(value) {

  1928:   return Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) ? null : date;
